## Contradicted Decisions
None. [0010-manual-lifecycle-git] (tracked `Change.Status` uses the leading
letter of Git's name-status code; untracked paths use `?`) is implemented as
specified and no eval contradicts it. Rename entries are reported under the
new path with `OldPath` retained from the name-status line.

## Gaps
- GIT-005 — the spec does not say whether a supplied `Request.Env` replaces the
  child environment or extends the inherited one. Implemented as inherit +
  override (supplied values win). Target module: gitstore.
- GIT-005 — behavior when a grandchild escapes the launched process group
  (forked between the readiness write and the launch-time cancellation) and
  keeps the stdout/stderr pipes open is unspecified. `Run` terminates the
  process group and bounds the wait for the captured pipes (`WaitDelay`),
  returning the `context.Canceled`/`context.DeadlineExceeded` error; escaping
  daemonized processes remain a documented limit. Target module: gitstore.
- GIT-005 — exit code reported when the process is killed by a signal, and the
  meaning of `Request.Timeout == 0`, are unspecified. Implemented as -1 for
  signal-killed processes and as "no timeout, context still honored" for a
  zero `Timeout`. Target module: gitstore.
- GIT-001 — the spec does not say what `Inspect` reports on a repository with
  no commits (unborn HEAD). Implemented as `Head == ""` with a nil error;
  non-repositories still error as required. Target module: gitstore.
- GIT-003 — merge policy when the staged and unstaged diffs disagree on status
  for the same path (keep a rename entry carrying `OldPath`, otherwise keep
  the first-seen entry) and change ordering are unspecified; only "once per
  reported Path" is mandated. Target module: gitstore.
- GIT-007 — spec is silent on whether `Commit` runs repository hooks or must
  honor `commit.gpgsign` (implemented via plumbing `commit-tree`/`update-ref`,
  which bypasses both) and on the result of an empty path selection
  (implemented as an error). Target module: gitstore.
- GIT-007 — which identity authors the commit when the repository config and
  environment lack `user.name`/`user.email` is unspecified. Target module:
  gitstore.
- GIT-006 — paths containing `:` are not representable with `rev:path` syntax
  and revision-resolution failure vs absent path is not distinguished; error
  text for either is unspecified. Target module: gitstore.
- GIT-002 — resolution of a base that is present only as a symref (e.g.
  `refs/remotes/origin/HEAD`) and of base names containing slashes is
  unspecified; the remote ref check accepts refs that resolve, matching the
  "locally known" wording. Target module: gitstore.
- GIT-009 — which object id an annotated tag ref reports (tag object vs peeled
  commit) is unspecified; implemented literally as the ref's own object id.
  Target module: gitstore.
- GIT-010 — the all-zeros expected-old sentinel is hard-coded for SHA-1's 40
  hex digits (zero new means delete; zero old means "ref must not exist");
  other object formats are out of scope per the drill conventions. Target
  module: gitstore.

**Found the root cause.** The fixture writes the `ready` file before forking `sleep`; there's an inherent race where cancellation lands between the `ready` write and the fork, so the eventual `sleep` child escapes the group SIGKILL and holds the stdout/stderr pipes open — blocking `cmd.Run()` until it exits naturally (5s). `exec.CommandContext`'s `WaitDelay` handles exactly this: it bounds how long `Wait` waits for pipe-holding children after cancellation.
Stable across repeated runs. Now run the target eval hook from the workspace root:
All 14 eval cases pass. Let me check what changed in the tree:
Done. Summary of the work:

**Rebuilt:** `packages/gitstore/internal/local/local.go` (the module's disposable path) from the spec, Decision [0010-manual-lifecycle-git], the retained interfaces (`api.go`, `model/**`) and the evals. No other paths touched; `.fireweed/drill-eval.xml` is the report the hook writes.

**Key implementation points:**
- **Inspect** — `rev-parse --show-toplevel` + `--git-common-dir`, symlink-canonicalized; branch via `symbolic-ref` (empty on detached/unborn HEAD); non-repo errors. Note: this Git build (2.30.1 Apple) doesn't support `--path-format=absolute`, so relative common-dir output is joined against the probe dir.
- **BaseRef** — origin default; remote HEAD symbolic-ref for the omitted base (else `main`); prefers `refs/remotes/<remote>/<base>`, falls back to `refs/heads/<base>`, errors otherwise.
- **Changes** — `git diff --cached -M -z <base>` + `git diff -M -z` + `ls-files -z --others --exclude-standard`, merged once per Path, renames keep `OldPath`, status = leading letter of name-status, `?` untracked.
- **WriteBlob / ReadBlob / WriteTree-based Commit** — plumbing (`hash-object`, `read-tree`/`write-tree`/`commit-tree`/`update-ref`) against a throwaway `GIT_INDEX_FILE` seeded from HEAD, so selected paths get worktree content while the real index, worktree and unrelated staged changes stay untouched; empty tree for unborn HEAD.
- **CASRef** — `git update-ref --no-deref <name> <new|zero> <old|zero>`; empty or all-zero old = must-not-exist (both create and delete), zero new = delete — verified against this Git's actual transaction semantics.
- **Run** — argv as-is, `Setpgid` + `kill(-pgid, SIGKILL)` on cancel/timeout, captured streams, nonzero exit/timeout/cancel are errors, `errors.Is` matches `context.DeadlineExceeded`, error text excludes streams.

**Notable find:** the cancel-during-launch eval's fixture writes the ready file before forking `sleep`, so a SIGKILL group can land while a grandchild is mid-fork; it survives, holds the captured pipes, and blocks `wait`. Fixed with launch-time group kill plus a bounded pipe wait (`WaitDelay: 500ms`), which returns the context error instead of hanging. Verified stable across many full-suite and focused runs.

**DRILL_NOTES.md** — `## Contradicted Decisions`: none (0010 implemented as specified). `## Gaps`: 11 gaps, each naming its nearest spec ID (GIT-001/002/003/005/006/007/009/010) and target module gitstore — including env merge semantics, the escaping-grandchild behavior, unborn-HEAD Inspect, hook/gpgsign bypass in Commit, and the SHA-1 zero-OID sentinel.

**Verification:** `go test ./packages/gitstore/evals/` passes; the eval hook reports **PASS** (14 JUnit cases). Two caveats: `go build ./...` fails on the pre-existing drill input's `main.go` (it references `packages/app`, which is intentionally absent from this input), and `.fireweed/drill-eval.xml` is the untracked report the hook produced — left in place for the drill harness.
